122026-07-31T04:08:29+00:00@ArtificialAnlys195,810MiniMax H3 is #1 in Video Editing and ranks top 3 in both Text to Video and Image to Video on Artificial Analysis. MiniMax plans to release its weights under the MiniMax Community License, which would make it the leading open weights model by far MiniMax H3 is the latest video model from MiniMax and the successor to their Hailuo family. The model has a multimodal architecture, accepting text, images, videos, and audio clips as input to generate 5-15s clips at 24fps with native audio generation. Video input support also makes MiniMax H3 one of the few models capable of instruction-based video editing, where it takes the #1 spot in our Video Editing Leaderboard. The model is also #2 in Text to Video, and #3 in Image to Video. MiniMax H3 is available now in the Hailuo AI app and via the MiniMax API. MiniMax lists MiniMax H3 at $0.13 per second of 2K video ($7.80 per minute), with a 768p tier at $0.09 per second ($5.40 per minute) marked as coming soon in their documentation. The first five reference images are free, with each additional image at $0.04 and reference audio free. Our evaluation was conducted on the 2K tier. At $7.80 per minute with audio, MiniMax H3 at 2K ($7.80/min) undercuts Dreamina Seedance 2.0 1080p ($22.45/min), HappyHorse-1.1 ($9.90/min), and Kling 3.0 1080p ($20.16/min), but is still at a premium over Google’s Gemini Omni Flash at $6.00/min. MiniMax plans to release the weights under the MiniMax Community License, which permits commercial use for organizations under $20M in revenue, subject to prominent attribution requirements. If released, MiniMax H3 would become the strongest open weights video model, well ahead of the previous open weights leader LTX-2.3. We’ll be sharing more insights into our evaluation soon - congratulations to @Hailuo_AI on the release! See below for comparisons between MiniMax H3 and other leading models in the Artificial Analysis Video Editing Arena 🧵中立H3 发布与能力介绍是 · 开放资格非 Goodcase打开帖子

672026-07-31T03:57:51+00:00@beginnersblog112,645I tested Hailuo Minimax H3. I was using storyboards to guide MiniMax H3. The results were inconsistent. The absurd event is filmed with a completely serious, unscripted documentary feeling. 0–3s: [Handheld medium shot] A middle-aged man wearing a dark business suit and tie crouches beside the stone edge of a pond. With a completely serious expression, he slowly scatters pieces of bread from a paper bag to several ordinary koi. Small ripples spread across the water as the fish gather in front of him. 3–7s: [Camera instinctively moves closer] An abnormally huge orange-and-white koi suddenly surges out of the murky water, briefly lifting its upper body above the surface and biting down on the entire bread bag in the man’s left hand. He freezes for half a second, then grips the bag with both hands and leans backward. Startled, the person filming steps back. The image briefly loses focus before locking onto the man and the giant fish again. 7–11s: [Close handheld action shot] The giant koi pulls violently toward the deeper part of the pond. The wet paper bag stretches, the man’s arms tense, and his leather shoes slide repeatedly across the wet stone. His knee strikes the edge of the pond. He tries to brace himself with his right foot, but the sole loses traction and his center of gravity moves past the edge. Water, pieces of bread, and fallen leaves scatter from the force as the camera operator hurriedly moves sideways. 11–15s: [Impact and final hold] The paper bag suddenly tears. The man loses all support and pitches forward into the pond, creating one heavy, realistic splash. The camera quickly tilts downward while keeping the center of the pond visible. The man resurfaces with duckweed covering his head and his wet tie stuck across his face. The giant koi calmly swims past him with the remains of the bread bag still in its mouth. The camera holds on the man’s stunned expression while the koi casually swims away beside him. Keep the man’s face, dark suit, tie, and paper bag visually consistent throughout. The koi must retain the same orange-and-white markings and enormous size. The pulling, sliding, loss of balance, and fall must show believable weight, inertia, traction, and water displacement. Natural park ambience and a realistic splash only. No dialogue, no subtitles, no music. Avoid cuts, character teleportation, changes in the fish’s size, extra limbs, and cartoonish acting. #MiniMaxH3 #AIVideo正面Seedance / 竞品横向对比是 · 竞品比较动作 / 特效动作表现、转场 / 叙事、运镜 / 动态无明确结论 · 动作表现、转场 / 叙事、运镜 / 动态打开帖子

1112026-07-31T10:39:49+00:00@huoshan0077,301兄弟们 MiniMax 这个 H3，有点不像普通图生视频了。 更像是把“图片动起来 + 动作迁移 + 配音 + 运镜”全塞进一个模型里。 玩法很简单： 给它一张人物图。 再丢一段动作视频。 再放一段声音。 它就能让图片里的人，跟着视频里的动作动起来，还能唱你放进去的声音。 人物、动作、运镜、声音，它是一起理解的。 这点挺狠。 之前做这种东西，基本是： 先图生视频。 再动作迁移。 再配音。 最后还要剪。 中间任何一步崩了，都得重来。 H3 这次相当于一个模型直接打包干完。 最长能生成 15 秒视频。 支持原生 2K。 还自带双声道声音。 价格也还行，2K 视频每秒 0.13 美元，生成 15 秒大概 1.95 美元。 这东西适合的场景很明显： AI 短剧。 电商广告。 商品视频。 音乐视频。 比如你手上有一张商品图，再找一段你喜欢的广告运镜，就可以让它按那个动作重新生成一条商品视频。 对做短视频的人来说，确实省很多折腾。 甚至可以拿去帮闲鱼商家、电商店铺、本地商家做产品视频。 这活儿以前麻烦得要死，现在门槛又被往下砍了一刀。 另外 MiniMax 后面还会开放 H3 的模型权重。 不过先别急着封神。 官方展示的一般都是挑过的好案例。 人物一致性、中文口型、复杂动作稳不稳，还得自己真跑几条才知道。 但方向已经很明显了。 视频生成这块，真开始卷到“一个模型干一整条片子”了。正面H3 发布与能力介绍是 · 潜在风险非 Goodcase打开帖子

1302026-07-31T02:58:58+00:00@vladimircherner5,457Minimax Hailuo 3 is officially out — and open source has a new king. Here's what's in the box 🧵 Specs: - 5–15 sec clips - Aspect ratios: 21:9, 16:9, 4:3, 1:1, 3:4, 9:16 + Auto - Resolution: SD and 2K. No 1080p, no 4K — it skips Full HD entirely and jumps straight to 2K - 24 FPS, H.264/AVC and H.265/HEVC, AAC or MP3 audio - Native stereo in every generation — rendered together with the picture, not as a separate pass. - Prompts up to 7,000 characters. Room to stretch out. What it can do: - References: up to 9 images, 3 videos, 3 audio files - Keyframes - Multishot - Video editing - Understands Ukrainian — not flawlessly, around 90% accuracy, but for a video model that's already good Pricing. Official site @Hailuo_AI : $1.50 for 15 sec, so $0.10/sec. The drone must follow the green guideline path as a smooth continuous move: begin near the left side of the interchange, rise and sweep across the middle highway corridor, continue through the right-side city edge, then climb higher into the skyline, perform a tight spiral/orbit near the upper tower cluster, descend back toward the interchange, curve around the large circular loop, and finally glide out toward the lower-right water edge. The circular loop becomes dominant again. Maintain realistic drone inertia and readable geography. 0:13–0:15 Curve around the large circular flyover loop and finish with a fast glide toward the lower-right over the water, ending on a dramatic monsoon reveal of wet infrastructure, dark sea, and rain-washed Mumbai. VISUAL STYLE Hyper-real cinematic realism, heavy monsoon rain, grey storm clouds, wet roads, reflective flyovers, rain haze, cool blue-grey morning light, dark sea, atmospheric depth, realistic Mumbai scale, premium documentary-film look. AUDIO No voiceover. No dialogue. Use heavy rainfall, rushing wind, distant thunder, soft drone motor whirr, faint traffic hum, sea movement, and subtle cinematic tension music building with the motion. 2272026-07-30T00:11:43+00:00@nemopi1,163🔥 MiniMax H3を先行テストしてみました！ 今回、Hailuoさんにお声がけいただき、 新しい動画生成モデル「MiniMax H3」のアーリーアクセステストに参加しています🎦 まずは、先日投稿して反響をいただいたダンス動画のプロンプトを短く整理して、 同じキーフレームからH3で生成してみました。 ※画像1枚＋プロンプトのみ 率直な感想は、 ダンスの動きがかなり自然です👍️ 最初のポーズから突然別の姿勢へ切り替わるのではなく、 腕、腰、脚の動きがつながりながら、 そのまま自然に踊り始める感じ。 膝や重心、肩、腰まで身体全体でリズムを取っていて、 単に手足を動かしているのではなく、 ちゃんと“踊っている”ように見えました💃 特に良かったのが、 髪やワイドパンツ、靴ひもなどが 身体の動きより少し遅れて揺れるところ。 動きが止まったあとにも余韻が残って、 短い映像なのにMVらしい空気が生まれています✨ 先日のバズったSeedance版は、 強い魚眼やローアングル、スニーカーが迫る大胆な画づくりが魅力でした。 一方で今回のH3版は、 細かな演出をそのまま再現するというより、 プロンプトの意図を読み取って、 自然で見やすいダンス映像へまとめてくれた印象です。 つまり、 Seedance版 → 構図や演出を細かく設計して作り込む MiniMax H3版 → 要点を渡して、自然な動きと映像のまとまりを引き出す という違いがありそうです。 短いプロンプトでも、 人物1人を保ちながら、 ここまで自然に動かせたのはかなり好印象でした☺️ 細かな魚眼演出や顔の固定は、 これからもう少し検証したいところですが、 まず触ってみた感触としてはかなり有望です👏 以前は、静止画の中にいたキャラクターが 少しずつ動き出すだけでも驚いていましたが、 それが今では、 重心を移し、髪や服を揺らしながら、 まるで最初からそこにいたように踊り始める😆 新しいモデルを試すたびに、 静止画と映像の境界が また少しだけ薄くなっていく気がします🎶 実用的で楽しみなモデルなので、 引き続き、H3ならではの表現を色々試してみます🫡 #MiniMaxH3 @Hailuo_AI正面Seedance / 竞品横向对比是 · 竞品比较音乐 / 舞蹈 / MV动作表现、提示词遵循、角色一致性各有优劣 · 动作表现、运镜 / 动态、提示词遵循打开帖子

2482026-07-31T11:23:11+00:00@LongboardFamara819Just finished Surfing Titans, a cinematic surf teaser I made to test how MiniMax H3 handles water physics, camera movement, continuity, and sound. My honest impression: H3 held up really well. Water is usually one of the fastest ways to expose an AI video model, but here the waves kept a convincing sense of weight, scale, and movement. The camera work also felt much more intentional than I expected, especially in the more dynamic shots. The sound surprised me too. It stayed coherent across the sequence and supported the visuals without falling apart or becoming distracting. I also made very few cuts. Most of the material was already usable, which made the edit feel much more like shaping a sequence than fixing broken generations. Still testing, still learning the model, but this was a very strong first result. 3082026-07-31T08:22:50+00:00@mrgris455Entre Flux 3, Seedance 2.5 et MiniMax H3 qui va sortir dans quelques heures (avec de l'opensource), l'IA générative vidéo est en pleine effervescence, à la lecture de l'annonce pour H3, c'est du très très lourd ! Petite synthèse : MiniMax a présenté Hailuo 3.0 (nom technique H3), sa nouvelle génération de modèle vidéo, dévoilée au WAIC 2026 le 17 juillet et en cours de déploiement sur Hailuo et les plateformes partenaires. À ne pas confondre avec M3, qui est leur modèle de texte : les deux ont été annoncés au même événement mais font des choses différentes, H3 étant purement dédié à la génération vidéo. Ce qui change vraiment : Côté image, on passe à de la 2K native en 24 images par seconde, avec des durées de 5 à 15 secondes par génération, extensibles à une trentaine de secondes, et des formats allant du 21:9 au 9:16. Le gros ajout, c'est l'audio synchronisé généré en une seule passe, plus un système de contrôle qu'ils appellent « omni-référence » : il accepte d'un coup jusqu'à 9 images de référence, 3 extraits vidéo et 3 extraits audio pour garder le style, le personnage, le mouvement et la voix cohérents. Il y a aussi de l'édition pilotée par instructions et une cohérence des personnages d'une scène à l'autre.正面H3 发布与能力介绍是 · 开放资格非 Goodcase无明确结论打开帖子

What stands out is how the model doesn't just generate video from text — it understands and combines text, images, video, and audio into a single unified workflow. Rather than chaining separate tools together, H3 operates as a complete creative engine, handling visuals, motion, and sound simultaneously. This represents a significant shift from the fragmented tooling that has defined AI video production so far. 🔹 @ShamiWeb3 2️⃣ SEEDANCE 2.5 BRINGS LONGER, HIGHER-QUALITY AI VIDEO GENERATIONS Seedance 2.5 is arriving soon on the ImagineArt platform, bringing longer generation lengths and higher-quality outputs with the kind of fine-grained control creators have been asking for. With this release, the model aims to give creators more reliable results without sacrificing resolution or coherence across extended sequences. 🔹 @jji1jj 3️⃣ PIXVERSE DEPTH MAP CONTROL SOLVES SCENE CONSISTENCY IN AI VIDEO PixVerse introduced Depth Map Control, a feature that tackles one of the hardest problems in AI video generation — keeping scenes spatially consistent across camera movement, object position, and depth relationships. By using a reference video to capture the scene's geometry, creators can maintain accurate spatial relationships while generating new content. This moves AI video beyond simple motion and toward controllable, cinematic-grade output where the camera actually behaves predictably. 🔹 @Nusaiba0_ 4️⃣ COMFYUI FULL PIPELINE: STORYBOARD TO FINAL RENDER IN ONE HOUR A developer built an entire anime-inspired 2D motion sequence entirely inside a single ComfyUI pipeline. 3582026-07-31T07:11:26+00:00@amandasaturnino213字节发布Seedance 2.5，延续多模态音视频联合生成架构，重点突破长叙事、多模态参考和视频编辑能力。 模型支持单次生成最长30秒视频，并支持多轮延长，能够在长视频中保持人物、场景、镜头以及音画的一致性，实现更完整的故事表达。同时，模型进一步升级多模态参考能力，支持最多30张图片、10段视频及10段音频输入，可综合理解人物、动作、风格、场景等信息，实现更加复杂、可控的视频创作。 相比此前AI视频模型主要关注画质和单镜头生成，Seedance 2.5 更加强调内容生产流程的完整性和商业化落地能力。通过时间戳精准编辑、绿幕编辑、视角调整、白模参考等能力，模型能够更好满足影视、广告、电商、游戏等专业场景需求，有望进一步降低内容制作成本，提升AI视频生产效率。 MiniMax 全模态生成模型 H3，侧重点更加偏向通用多模态能力建设。MiniMax H3支持文本、图像、视频、声音等多模态信息统一理解与生成，并具备原生音视频生成能力。 Seedance 2.5 和 MiniMax H3 分别代表了AI多模态发展的两个方向： 前者聚焦视频创作工业化，通过更强的视频生成和编辑能力推动应用落地； 后者强化通用全模态能力，通过开放生态扩大模型应用范围。 随着模型能力持续提升、成本不断下降，AI应用侧有望迎来新一轮景气周期，视频生成、智能创作、内容生产等场景是重点关注方向。 AI大模型：MiniMax、智谱、阿里巴巴、腾讯控股； Token运营及垂类数据：荣信文化、易点天下、中信出版、中国科传、世纪天鸿、新华网、人民网等； AI影视内容生成与多模态内容：荣信文化、中文在线、掌阅科技、昆仑万维、华策影视、博纳影视等； 全链路AI营销+电商：易点天下、汇量科技、值得买等； AI智能体解决方案：迈富时等中立Seedance / 竞品横向对比是 · 竞品比较非 Goodcase各有优劣 · 转场 / 叙事、多模态 / 音频、开放度打开帖子
